### User

_source line 1 · 2026-05-25T08:57:08.529Z_

You need to transfer files to a remote server using an MCP SSH tool.

**Task**: Read the file `/tmp/paper_transfer/cmd_group2.sh` and execute its contents on the remote server `westd-11235` using the `mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute` tool with `account="root"`.

The .sh file contains bash commands (base64 decode heredocs) that will write experiments.tex and references.bib to the remote server.

Steps:
1. Read `/tmp/paper_transfer/cmd_group2.sh` to get the command content
2. Call `mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute(server="westd-11235", command=&lt;contents of the .sh file>, account="root")`
3. Verify by calling `mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_read_only(server="westd-11235", command="ls -la /root/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/experiments.tex /root/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/references.bib", account="root")`

Report whether both files were successfully written with correct sizes (experiments.tex ~17KB, references.bib ~22KB).
